\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}<:(\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}})\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

By Lemma
B.1
,
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathbf{Unit}
.

By rule
ST-LetUnit
,
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{1}\mathit{B}
.

This case follows by setting
\Gamma^{\prime}:=\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

•

Rule
ST-Inj1
. Have
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

Need to show:
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{1}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}
.

By IH,
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{1}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

This case follows by setting
\Gamma^{\prime}:=\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

•

Rule
ST-Inj2
. Similar to rule
ST-Inj1
.

•

Rule
ST-Case
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
and
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:1
.

Need to show:
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}
.

By IH,
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}},\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{22}}}
and
\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{1}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}<:(\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}})\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{22}}}
and
\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Since
q\neq 0
, therefore
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{q^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

The remainder of the proof for this case is different for
\mathbb{N}_{=}
and
\mathbb{N}_{\geq}
.

–

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{=}
, since
<:
is discrete,
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}=\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{22}}}=\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
(say).

Then,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
. Further, by rule
ST-SubL
,
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
.

By Lemma
B.1
,
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by setting
\Gamma^{\prime}:=\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

–

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{\geq}
,
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}}
may not be equal to
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{22}}}
. So we need to employ the following construction. For quantities
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
define an operator:
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}};\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
if
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}<:\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
otherwise.
Extending the operator to contexts
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
where
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\rfloor
, define
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}:=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}};\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
as
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
and
\overline{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}}=\overline{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}};\overline{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
(defined pointwise).

Now, let
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}:=\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{21}}};\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{22}}}
. Then,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Again, by rule
ST-SubL
,
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
.

By Lemma
B.1
,
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by setting
\Gamma^{\prime}:=\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

•

Rule
ST-SubL
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma<:\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

Need to show:
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{1}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}
.

By IH,
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{1}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

This case, then, follows by setting
\Gamma^{\prime}:=\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

•

Rule
ST-SubR
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q^{\prime}}}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
and
\mathit{q}<:\mathit{q^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{1}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma<:\mathit{q^{\prime}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Since
\mathit{q^{\prime}}\neq 0
, therefore
\mathit{q}\neq 0
.

By IH,
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{1}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma<:\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

Now, since
\mathit{q}<:\mathit{q^{\prime}}
, therefore
\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}<:\mathit{q^{\prime}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

This case, then, follows by setting
\Gamma^{\prime}:=\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
.

∎

Lemma B.3 (Splitting (Lemma
3.3
)).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\mathit{A}
, then there exists
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Proof.

If
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}=0
, then
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}:=0\cdot\Gamma
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}:=\Gamma
.

Otherwise, by Lemma
B.2
,
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{1}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma<:(\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Then,
\Gamma=(\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}
for some
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}
(in case of
\mathbb{N}_{=}
, we have,
\overline{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}}=\overline{0}
).

Now, by Lemma
B.1
,
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}}\mathit{A}
and
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\mathit{A}
.

By rule
ST-SubL
,
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}}\mathit{A}
.

The lemma follows by setting
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}:=\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}:=\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{0}}}
.
∎

Lemma B.4 (Weakening (Lemma
3.4
)).

If
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
, then
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{0}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.
∎

Lemma B.5 (Substitution (Lemma
3.5
)).

If
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor
, then
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

•

Rule
ST-Var
. There are three cases to consider.

–

0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{0}\mathit{C},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{0}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma,0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{=}
, we have,
\overline{\Gamma}=\overline{0}
. This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Var
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{\geq}
, this case follows by rule
ST-Var
and rule
ST-SubL
.

–

0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
where
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma,0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

Follows by lemma
B.4
.

–

0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}},\mathit{z}:^{0}\mathit{C},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{r}}\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{0}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
and
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\mathit{x}:^{(\mathit{q}+\mathit{r})}\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{=}
, we have,
\overline{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}}=\overline{0}
and
\overline{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}}=\overline{0}
and
r=0
. This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Var
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{\geq}
, this case follows by rule
ST-Var
and rule
ST-SubL
.

•

Rule
ST-Lam
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{r}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{r}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-Lam
.

•

Rule
ST-App
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}^{\mathit{r}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
B.3
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-App
.

•

Rule
ST-Pair
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}):^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By lemma
B.3
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Pair
.

•

Rule
ST-LetPair
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
B.3
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-LetPair
.

•

Rule
ST-Unit
. Have:
0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{0}\mathit{C},0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{unit}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathbf{Unit}
. Also
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{0}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma,0\cdot\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{unit}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathbf{Unit}
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{=}
, we have,
\overline{\Gamma}=\overline{0}
. This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Unit
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{\geq}
, this case follows by rule
ST-Unit
and rule
ST-SubL
.

•

Rule
ST-LetUnit
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
B.3
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-LetUnit
.

•

Rule
ST-Inj1
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Inj1
.

•

Rule
ST-Inj2
. Similar to rule
ST-Inj1
.

•

Rule
ST-Case
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}+\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
B.3
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Case
.

•

Rule
ST-SubL
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}<:\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{r^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}<:\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

Since
\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{r^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
, by rule
ST-SubR
,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
.

By IH,
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-SubL
.

•

Rule
ST-SubR
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q^{\prime}}}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
and
\mathit{q}<:\mathit{q^{\prime}}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{r_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q^{\prime}}}\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-SubR
.

∎

Theorem B.6 (Preservation (Theorem
3.6
)).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
and
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
, then
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a^{\prime}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
and inversion on
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

•

Rule
ST-App
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}
.

Let
\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}
, when
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-App
.

–

\mathit{b}=\lambda^{\mathit{r}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A^{\prime}}.\mathit{b^{\prime}}
and
\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}\{\mathit{a}/\mathit{x}\}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}\{\mathit{a}/\mathit{x}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

By inversion on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{r}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A^{\prime}}.\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
, we get
\mathit{A^{\prime}}=\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{B}
for some
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{q}
.

Now, there are two cases to consider.

*

\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}=0
. Since
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}<:\mathit{q}
, so
\mathit{q}=0
.

Then, by the substitution lemma,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}\{\mathit{a}/\mathit{x}\}:^{0}\mathit{B}
.

*

\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\neq 0
. By lemma
B.2
,
\exists\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\mathit{r^{\prime}}
such that
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{r^{\prime}}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}<:\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r}<:\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r^{\prime}}
.

Since
\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\neq 0
, therefore
\mathit{r}<:\mathit{r^{\prime}}
. Hence, by rule
ST-SubL
,
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{1}\mathit{B}
.

Now, by lemma
B.1
,
\mathit{q}\cdot\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
. By rule
ST-SubL
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by the substitution lemma.

•

Rule
ST-LetPair
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Let
\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}
, when
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-LetPair
.

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}\}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}\}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}\}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

By inversion on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, we have:

\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}
.

This case, then, follows by applying the substitution lemma twice.

•

Rule
ST-LetUnit
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Let
\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}
, when
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-LetUnit
.

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{=}
, we have,
\overline{\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}}=\overline{0}
. This case, then, follows directly from the premise,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

In case of
\mathbb{N}_{\geq}
, this case follows by rule
ST-SubL
.

•

Rule
ST-Case
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Let
\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, when
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-Case
.

–

\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\}
.

Need to show
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}+\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

By inversion on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, we have,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by applying the substitution lemma.

–

\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathbf{inj}_{2}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}
.

Similar to the previous case.

•

Rules
ST-SubL
and
ST-SubR
. Follows by IH.

∎

Theorem B.7 (Progress (Theorem
3.7
)).

If
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
, then either
\mathit{a}
is a value or there exists
\mathit{a^{\prime}}
such that
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{A}
.

•

Rule
ST-Var
. Does not apply since the context here is empty.

•

Rule
ST-App
. Have:
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A}
.

Need to show:
\exists c,\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}\leadsto\mathit{c}
.

By IH,
\mathit{b}
is either a value or
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}
.

If
\mathit{b}
is a value, then
\mathit{b}=\lambda^{\mathit{r}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b^{\prime}}
for some
\mathit{b^{\prime}}
. Therefore,
\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}\{\mathit{a}/\mathit{x}\}
.

Otherwise,
\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{r}}
.

•

Rule
ST-LetPair
. Have:
\emptyset\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}{}^{\mathit{r}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\cdot\mathit{r}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Need to show:
\exists c,\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
.

By IH,
\mathit{a}
is either a value or
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

If
\mathit{a}
is a value, then
\mathit{a}=(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}})
. Therefore,

\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}\}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}\}
.

Otherwise,
\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}
.

•

Rule
ST-LetUnit
. Have:
\emptyset\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Need to show:
\exists c,\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
.

By IH,
\mathit{a}
is either a value or
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

If
\mathit{a}
is a value, then
\mathit{a}=\mathbf{unit}
. Therefore,

\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b}
.

Otherwise,
\vdash\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathbf{let}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}
.

•

Rule
ST-Case
. Have:
\emptyset\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
where
\emptyset\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
and
\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}\cdot\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{q}}\mathit{B}
.

Need to show:
\exists c,\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{c}
.

By IH,
\mathit{a}
is either a value or
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

If
\mathit{a}
is a value, then
\mathit{a}=\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
or
\mathit{a}=\mathbf{inj}_{2}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Then,
\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\}
or
\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\}
.

Otherwise,
\vdash\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathbf{case}_{\mathit{q_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

•

Rules
ST-SubL
and
ST-SubR
. Follows by IH.

•

Rules
ST-Lam
,
ST-Pair
, and
ST-Unit
. The terms typed by these rules are values.

∎

Appendix C
Dependency Analysis in Simply-Typed LDC

Lemma C.1 (Multiplication (Lemma
3.8
)).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
, then
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

•

Rule
ST-VarD
. Have:
\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Need to show:
\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A}
.

This case follows by rule
ST-VarD
.

•

Rule
ST-LamD
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma,\mathit{x}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma,\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>(\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m})}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-LamD
using associativity of
\sqcup
.

•

Rule
ST-AppD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>(\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m})}\mathit{A}
.

By rule
ST-AppD
, using associativity of
\sqcup
,
(\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}})\sqcap(\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Now, for elements
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{3}}}
of any lattice,
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcup\>(\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\>\sqcap\>\ell_{{\mathrm{3}}})\sqsubseteq(\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}})\>\sqcap\>(\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell_{{\mathrm{3}}})
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-SubLD
, using the above relation.

•

Rule
ST-PairD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>(\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m})}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By rule
ST-PairD
, using associativity of
\sqcup
,
(\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}})\sqcap(\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

For
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}},\ell_{{\mathrm{3}}}
, we have,
\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcup\>(\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}\>\sqcap\>\ell_{{\mathrm{3}}})\sqsubseteq(\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}})\>\sqcap\>(\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell_{{\mathrm{3}}})
.

This case follows by rule
ST-SubLD
, using the above relation.

•

Rule
ST-LetPairD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>(\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m})}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case follows by rules
ST-LetPairD
and
ST-SubLD
, using associativity of
\sqcup
and the distributive inequality.

•

Rule
ST-UnitD
. Have:
\top\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{unit}:^{\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
.

Need to show:
\top\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{unit}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
.

Follows by rule
ST-UnitD
.

•

Rule
ST-LetUnitD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case follows by rules
ST-LetUnitD
and
ST-SubLD
, using the distributive inequality.

•

Rule
ST-Inj1D
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Inj1D
.

•

Rule
ST-Inj2D
. Similar to rule
ST-Inj1D
.

•

Rule
ST-CaseD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup(\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}})\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
and
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case follows by rules
ST-CaseD
and
ST-SubLD
, using the distributive inequality.

•

Rule
ST-SubLD
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\sqsubseteq\Gamma^{\prime}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Since
\Gamma\sqsubseteq\Gamma^{\prime}
, so
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\sqsubseteq\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma^{\prime}
.

This case follows by rule
ST-SubLD
.

•

Rule
ST-SubRD
. Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\ell^{\prime}
.

Need to show:
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqcup\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Since
\ell\sqsubseteq\ell^{\prime}
, so
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell\sqsubseteq\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell^{\prime}
.

This case follows by rule
ST-SubRD
.

∎

Lemma C.2 (Splitting (Lemma
3.9
)).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcap\>\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{A}
, then there exists
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.

Proof.

Have:
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}\>\sqcap\>\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{A}
. By rule
ST-SubRD
,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\mathit{A}
. The lemma follows by setting
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}:=\Gamma
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}:=\Gamma
.
∎

Lemma C.3 (Weakening (Lemma
3.10
)).

If
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
, then
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\top}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.
∎

Lemma C.4 (Substitution (Lemma
3.11
)).

If
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor
, then
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

•

Rule
ST-VarD
. There are three cases to consider.

–

\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\top}\mathit{C},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\top}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma,\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Follows by rule
ST-VarD
and rule
ST-SubLD
.

–

\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
where
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma,\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Follows by lemma
C.3
.

–

\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}},\mathit{z}:^{\top}\mathit{C},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\top}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
and
\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\mathit{x}:^{(\ell\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m})}\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{x}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Follows by rule
ST-VarD
and rule
ST-SubLD
.

•

Rule
ST-LamD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A}.\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-LamD
.

•

Rule
ST-AppD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}^{\mathit{m}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
C.2
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-AppD
.

•

Rule
ST-PairD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}^{\mathit{r}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}):^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By lemma
C.2
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-PairD
.

•

Rule
ST-LetPairD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
C.2
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-LetPairD
.

•

Rule
ST-UnitD
. Have:
\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\top}\mathit{C},\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{unit}:^{\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
. Also
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\top}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma,\top\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{unit}:^{\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
.

Follows by rule
ST-UnitD
and rule
ST-SubLD
.

•

Rule
ST-LetUnitD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
C.2
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-LetUnitD
.

•

Rule
ST-Inj1D
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-Inj1D
.

•

Rule
ST-Inj2D
. Similar to rule
ST-Inj1D
.

•

Rule
ST-CaseD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}\>\sqcap\>\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
C.2
,
\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{01}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{02}}}}}\mathit{C}
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{31}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{21}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{32}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{22}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-CaseD
.

•

Rule
ST-SubLD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Since
\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\sqsubseteq\mathit{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}
, by rule
ST-SubRD
,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
.

By IH,
\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-SubLD
.

•

Rule
ST-SubRD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{A}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{z}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
and
\ell\sqsubseteq\ell^{\prime}
. Also,
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{c}:^{\mathit{m_{{\mathrm{0}}}}}\mathit{C}
where
\lfloor\Gamma\rfloor=\lfloor\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\rfloor
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell^{\prime}}\mathit{A}
.

By IH,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma,\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}\{\mathit{c}/\mathit{z}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

This case, then, follows by rule
ST-SubRD
.

∎

Lemma C.5 (Restricted Upgrading).

If
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
and
\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqsubseteq\ell
, then
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Proof.

By Lemma
C.1
,
\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A},\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\>\sqcup\>\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Since
\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqsubseteq\ell
,
\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A},\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By rule
ST-SubLD
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
because
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\sqsubseteq\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqcup\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}
.
∎

Theorem C.6 (Preservation (Theorem
3.12
)).

If
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
and
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
, then
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a^{\prime}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
.

Proof.

By induction on
\Gamma\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A}
and inversion on
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

•

Rule
ST-AppD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}
.

Let
\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}
, when
\vdash\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-AppD
.

–

\mathit{b}=\lambda^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A^{\prime}}.\mathit{b^{\prime}}
and
\vdash\mathit{b}\>\mathit{a}^{\mathit{m}}\leadsto\mathit{b^{\prime}}\{\mathit{a}/\mathit{x}\}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}\{\mathit{a}/\mathit{x}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By inversion on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\lambda^{\mathit{m}}\mathit{x}:\mathit{A^{\prime}}.\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A}\to\mathit{B}
, we get
\mathit{A^{\prime}}=\mathit{A}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}}\mathit{B}
for some
\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\sqsubseteq\ell
.

By rule
ST-SubRD
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell_{{\mathrm{0}}}\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By lemma
C.5
,
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A}\vdash\mathit{b^{\prime}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

This case, then, follows by the substitution lemma.

•

Rule
ST-LetPairD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}},\mathit{y}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Let
\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}
, when
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-LetPairD
.

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}\>(\mathit{x}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{y})\>\mathbf{be}\>(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}})\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}\}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}\}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x}\}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}/\mathit{y}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By inversion on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash(\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}^{\mathit{m}},\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}):^{\ell}{}^{\mathit{m}}\!\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>\times\>\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, we have:

\exists\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}},\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}
such that
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell\>\sqcup\>\mathit{m}}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}=\Gamma_{{\mathrm{11}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{12}}}
.

This case, then, follows by applying the substitution lemma twice.

•

Rule
ST-LetUnitD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathbf{Unit}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Let
\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}
, when
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-LetUnitD
.

–

\vdash\mathbf{let}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{be}\>\mathbf{unit}\>\mathbf{in}\>\mathit{b}\leadsto\mathit{b}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by rule
ST-SubLD
.

•

Rule
ST-CaseD
. Have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
where
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
and
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}},\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Let
\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{c}
. By inversion:

–

\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, when
\vdash\mathit{a}\leadsto\mathit{a^{\prime}}
.

Need to show:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathbf{case}\>\mathit{a^{\prime}}\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

Follows by IH and rule
ST-CaseD
.

–

\vdash\mathbf{case}\>(\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}})\>\mathbf{of}\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\>;\>\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{2}}}}.\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{2}}}}\leadsto\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\}
.

Need to show
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\sqcap\Gamma_{{\mathrm{2}}}\vdash\mathit{b_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\{\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}/\mathit{x_{{\mathrm{1}}}}\}:^{\ell}\mathit{B}
.

By inversion on
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathbf{inj}_{1}\>\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}+\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{2}}}}
, we have:
\Gamma_{{\mathrm{1}}}\vdash\mathit{a_{{\mathrm{1}}}}:^{\ell}\mathit{A_{{\mathrm{1}}}}
.

This case, then, follows by applying the substitution lemma.

–
